Matching tricks found 2026-10-17 (try these FIRST on any near-miss):
  * Struct-copy register/spill order: an 8-byte member as 'struct { int a[2]; }' copies lwz a->r3, lwz b->r0, stw, stw (a two-int struct copies a,b one at a time; long long swaps halves). A CSE'd copy address (kept in a callee-saved reg) vs a recomputed 'addi rX,base,off': write the second use through a byte offset from a containing member ('(char*)&s->sub + 21') (SNDSYS_setopts).
  * Float compare operand order: `if (f)` (truthiness) gives `fcmpu f,zero`; `f != 0.0f` in either spelling gives `fcmpu zero,f` (iSirenEventProcessFnc).
  * A call result held across `new X(result, ...)` that goes `mr r0,r3; ...; mr rN,r0` instead of a direct `mr rN,r3`: wrap the new-expression in a static inline helper that takes the value (TibTextProcessFnc).
  * CVector3 copy ctor as an init-list union copy `CVector3(const CVector3& o) : d(o.d) {}` stays builtin (lfd,lfd,stfd,stfd) at any inline depth; `d = o.d` / `*(Pair*)this = ...` become operator= calls past the depth limit (DoBeginUpdate).
  * A register swap between a list-insert prev and the allocated node that no declaration or statement order fixes: make the insert an inline member taking the end/position as a PARAMETER (ServiceDataStreamer 46 -> 0); a param copy changes the colouring.
  * Scheduling barrier between a pointer computation and a following loop guard: write it as two statements (`p = (long*)area; n = ...; p -= n;`) - the permuter found an empty `if (!x) {}` (junk) for the same effect (ExPPC_PopStackFrame).
  * Squared lengths kept in callee-saved FPRs while only the sqrtf is recomputed = locals CW can CSE: a union-member CVector3 (`: d(o.d)` copy, d.v[i] access) instead of pointer-cast copies, which make the locals look aliased (SwapOut 628 -> 0).
  * A bool computed into a variable as `(a-b | b-a) >> 31` (not kept as a compare): the inline says `if (a != b) return true; return false;` (TibTextDraw IsMultiLine); a ctor storing z, y, x in reverse gives reversed temporary stores.
  * Callee-saved swap of a member-pointer local vs a loop temp: read the member through an inline
    accessor ('X* p = GetX();') - GetLookMatrix 2a57b7f6.
  * STACK SLOTS BY INLINE DEPTH: a temporary created inside a deeper inline sits LOWER (Soldier Init:
    SetPos arg moved from top to bottom via 'void SetTranslation(const CVector3& p) { SetPos(p); }').
  * Unexplained dead copy at the BOTTOM of the frame = a value passed/returned through an inline wrapper
    ('inline CVector3 SphereCenter(const CVolSphere& s) { return s.GetCenter(); }', 3f70bc13).
  * CW -inline auto limit counts PRE-optimisation source size (dead code too): if the orig does NOT inline
    a small earlier function into its caller, keep them in different units rather than inventing code.
  * STACK SLOTS: named locals go top-down in declaration order (first = highest), temps below them;
    an INLINE function's locals go bottom-up (first declared = lowest). Orig slots in 'reverse' order
    => the body is an inline helper (GetAzimuth 01afe7ce). Probe: scratch/.../probe.py idea, compile tiny f().
  * Two copies of a loop after an 'if (mp)' = ONE source loop with the if inside (CW unswitches).
  * Unroll factor follows IR size: per-axis locals / extra statements change x8 -> x4.
  * FP operand order: 'x = a; x *= k;' (compound assign keeps the variable as first operand).
  * FPR swap of two values computed in sequence: declare the LATER one before the earlier read
    ('float top; float scaleY = g.m_scaleY; top = 396.0f * scaleY;' - DrawStatusScreen fb3367cb).
  * A kept 'x * 1.0f' multiply = the 1.0 passed through an inline (CW folds a literal *1.0f): player_bullet.
  * BY-VALUE CVector3 temps built IN PLACE *and* dead param copies of an inlined by-value ctor at the frame
    bottom (CLine3(start,end)): CVector3 user copy ctor copying doublewords ONE BY ONE
    "{ d.pair[0] = o.d.pair[0]; d.pair[1] = o.d.pair[1]; }" (implicit copy: no in-place; "d = o.d": no dead copies).
    Unparked Respawn fdd4c0ac. Named start/end locals vs temporaries decides their slots.
  * Byte-swapped words stored to a stack slot TWICE (raw, then swapped) = the address-taking template
    ChangeEndian(T&) on an unsigned local (sound_endian.cpp helpers); "cmpwi 1; beq; b end" = one-case switch.
  * Several member loads all before their stores, in REVERSE order = an inline setter taking the values as
    arguments (CW evaluates arguments right to left): Float3::Set(x, y, z) in player Init 36bb2dc3.
  * Vector locals whose components orig keeps in callee-saved FPRs across calls: CVector3 as a union
    {double pair[2]; float v[4];} with IMPLICIT copies (a user copy ctor takes the address, blocking CSE).
  * Two dead stack stores of a pushed value = by-value small struct entry + by-value operator=; the count
    reload = the capacity check in a separate inline (Full()) from the store helper (hierobject 870b3845).
  * Literal multiplier in the "wrong" operand order (CW canonicalises literal operands): pass it through a
    plain float local ("float scale = 0.5f; x * scale") - CreatePlayer dcbd367a (permuter found it).
  * VOLATILE register numbering also follows declaration order (earlier declared = lower rN): hoist the
    loop locals to function scope in the orig order (anim_callback_finish db744e49: turn, maximum, info, i).
  * "v += K * w" with separate fmuls/fadds (no fmadds) and x first = a Scale(w, K) member into a local then
    +=; the 3-float-ctor operator* evaluates z first (CreateEjectedShell b75fc90d).
  * Reversed z,y,x loads of a copied vector: either an inline Set(x,y,z) (args right to left) or temps; the
    FPR numbers then follow DECLARATION order ("float x; float z = p.z; float y = p.y; x = p.x;"), and inside
    an inline the numbering is REVERSED (declare y, z, x there) - PrepareForAvoidance 32df9986.
  * Callee-saved numbering sometimes runs the OTHER way (later declared = higher): when the "earlier = higher"
    order fails, try the reverse; hoisting declarations to the top and assigning later lets you set it freely
    (GCHW_VD::iDraw 91f98ba7). A "rlwinm. 28,31,31" flag test (shift form) = a bitfield, "& 0x10" gives the mask form.
  * Weak vtable/dtor of a class with no key function: make its last virtual inline so CW emits the weak dtor
    here; discard the weak table/other weak copies with --weak-duplicate if their order differs (eec868df).
  * A scalar argument loaded BEFORE the by-value struct copies of the same call = it was read into a local
    first (CW copies aggregate args before evaluating a scalar member read): LoadLevelSettings bc998c05.
  * Views with virtual dtors in a fragment: declare 'virtual ~Derived();' or CW emits implicit dtors/vtables.
  * Two stack vectors in reverse slot order vs their code order = DECLARE them in slot order (first declared = highest); a cross product
    written straight into the destination (void Cross(a,b) setting x,y,z) avoids a w-copy of a garbage FPR (Soldier_object_bullet).
  * "li rX,14; beq; li rX,15" (not branchless neg/or/srwi) = "int k = 14; if (b) k = 15;" (int default then override).
  * Position copy z/y FPR swap: 'float y; float z = p.z; y = p.y; float x = p.x;' (y declared first).
  * A stack temp CW propagates away: take its address ('dst = *(T*)&tmp;'); NOT an empty dtor
    (that adds an EH cleanup action + out-of-line dtor; one_unit catches the extab).
  * Dead 16-byte copy: 'const CVector3& row = m.GetRow(1); CVector3 v(*&row);'.
  * Result kept in a stack short: 'short r; short* p = &r; ... else *p = 0; return *p;'.
  * Empty-looking ctr loop = a loop whose body only feeds an unused local.
  * Declaration order sets callee-saved numbering; 'int x; if (c) x = a; else x = b;' vs ternary.
  * Value-returning inline with 'if (a != b && !f()) return false; return true;' for bne/li/b shapes.
  * '(a + 1) * 10 + (b + 1)' parenthesised to stop strength reduction; 'int m = mission - 1;' locals
    to keep -1 out of displacements.

## STRATEGY (user-approved 2026-10-07, evening) - work in this order
The cheap tail (small const-free fragments, weak header copies) is nearly
exhausted. Do NOT spend loop iterations mining <200 B functions unless they
unblock something. Order:

1. TOOLING: pooled constants/strings - DONE (ab486ed). Usage:
   port_unit ... --pool .sdata2 [--pool .rodata]; manifest gets pooled_sections
   + pool_references (compiled section/offset/size -> original address); each
   item must be one whole compiler object (@N) equal to the original bytes.
   Limit: code that CSEs one pool base + immediate offsets (PlayGalleryMovie
   strings at base+2088..) still needs the whole pool layout - skip those.
   scratch/one_unit.py <id> runs one unit incl. check_pool (fast check).
   NOW: land the first-proof list below, then Game.md "pooled" blockers
   (grep -n -i pool docs/Game.md): bsmachin property readers, RestartPlayer,
   GetMuzzleFlashStatus, GetParticleAlpha/Size, MUSIC_Fade, PlayerScreenFlash,
   player camera-shake, thrown_obj ctor, AISplinePath Allocate (string base?).
   (Original design notes follow.)
   Goal: a fragment whose compiled code references pool entries (@N floats/
   doubles in .sdata2, strings in .rodata/.data pools) links those entries at
   their ORIGINAL pool addresses when the compiled bytes are identical to the
   original entry bytes. Data bytes stay uncounted (as always); code is still
   compiled from source and verified byte-for-byte, relocations included.
   Before starting: read docs/initial-audit.md and docs/Progress.md; look at
   how input_alignment (015f040) and weak_duplicates (3e3acf9) were added to
   tools/port_unit.py / tools/project_build.py / manifests. Design sketch:
   - port_unit pairs each compiled pool object (@N in .sdata2/.rodata/.data)
     with the original object at the relocation's target address, checks the
     bytes are equal, and records it in the manifest (e.g. "pool_externals":
     {compiled symbol -> original address}) instead of placing a section.
   - project_build links those symbols at the original addresses (absolute
     symbols) and verifies the original bytes there equal the compiled ones;
     any mismatch fails the unit. Never count the pool bytes.
   - Strings: same, matched by content at the relocation target (CW may merge
     identical strings; handle offsets into a pool object).
   - Tests in tests/ (positive + a mutation that changes a constant must fail),
     docs (docs/Progress.md / initial-audit as appropriate), then a full
     `python3 tools/reconstruct.py` + tests before landing via land.sh.
   First proofs once it works: PlayShellSound (ShellMenu, @2444 behind dead
   @726), SetupAudioSettings (@2943/@3702/@3703), PlayGalleryMovie
   (...rodata.0 strings), std::sqrtf in player.cpp, CScene ctor (rodata names),
   CPlayerWeaponObject::Shoot (@1116/@1114), Moh2 GetPosition/Get*ward
   (@2321/@1436), matrix.cpp Transform/Scale/Rotate*.
2. After the tooling lands: work the biggest files top-down (bsbifunc, player,
   ShellMenu, AIObject, Soldier_object, objcreate, scene, Moh2...), function
   runs 200-1000 B first, extending existing units where contiguous.
   Use `python3 tools/dependencies.py` (Roadmap Part 3) to prefer shared
   helpers with many unfinished callers when choosing between candidates.
3. Matching acceleration (Roadmap Phase D): decomp-permuter SET UP 2026-10-07.
   ~/tools/decomp-permuter (git clone, 8556c81) + ~/tools/permuter-venv (uv:
   toml, Levenshtein). Harness: scratch/perm/mkperm.py <dir> <prefix.cpp>
   <func.c> <start> <end> [tmpl] -> dir with compile.sh (prefix C++ views +
   extern "C" C function perm_fn taking self), target.o (original bytes with
   .reloc re-expressed). prefix.cpp: declarations ONLY (no other function
   bodies, they get scored). func.c: 'typedef struct X X;' lines + perm_fn.
   C sources (EA/MSL): scratch/perm/cperm.py <dir> <src> <mangled fn> [tmpl]
   (strips comments/other bodies, extern "C" kept via markers, typedefs
   for pycparser via PERM_PRETEND).
   Run: scratch/perm/run.sh <dir> -j8 --stop-on-zero (PATH has objdump and a
   pass-through cpp shim, scratch/perm/bin/cpp). Results in <dir>/output-*.
4. Ghidra (Roadmap Phase C): INSTALLED 2026-10-07 (user OK). Ghidra 12.1.4 +
   Temurin JDK 21 in ~/tools (checksums verified), Cuyler36 GameCubeLoader
   1.3.1 (Gekko/Broadway language, paired singles) in
   ~/tools/ghidra_12.1.4_PUBLIC/Ghidra/Extensions (version field set 12.1.4).
   Analyzed project: scratch/ghidra/proj (Frontline/Moh2RelGC.elf, language
   PowerPC:BE:32:Gekko_Broadway, mangled symbols from the ELF).
   Decompile: `sh scratch/ghidra/decomp.sh scratch/ghidra/out/x.c 0xADDR ...`
   (~1 s per call; script scratch/ghidra/scripts/DumpDecomp.java). Use it to
   draft big functions (types/fields still need evidence; Ghidra names like
   param_1/local_28 are not original).
   MCP: pyghidra-mcp 0.2.7 (stdio) registered in Claude Code at LOCAL scope
   (~/.claude.json, not in the repo) as `pyghidra-mcp`; launcher
   ~/tools/pyghidra-mcp.sh (JDK + GHIDRA_INSTALL_DIR), uv venv
   ~/tools/pyghidra-mcp-venv with Ghidra's bundled pyghidra 3.1.0 wheel.
   It opens a COPY of the project, scratch/ghidra/mcp/Frontline.gpr, so it

## Gotchas (things a fresh session must know)
- port_unit SILENTLY OVERWRITES config/GMFE69/<id>.json: before porting a new unit, check the id is unused (`ls config/GMFE69/<id>.json`), not just the source name. 2026-10-09 I clobbered the TRK mainloop.c unit with mainloop.cpp (fixed: id mainloop_game). Unit ids may differ from the source basename.
- Container views (2026-10-07): dwi::fast_vec<T> {T* data; int u4; int size;
  int capacity; bool owned; int growth} (24 B); dwi::list<T> {int count; int u4;
  node* pool; node* free; node head (prev,next,T); bool owned}; dwi::vector<T>
  {int size; int capacity; T* data; bool owned}; vec_iter {T* p}, dtor sets 0;
  hash_map {value_type* data; int size; int count}, freed from data-1.
  Weak template members: define OUT of class with __declspec(weak) and
  `template class X<T>;` per unit, defining only that unit's members (CW
  rejects member explicit instantiation; in-class inline members are not
  emitted). Emission order quirks: probe with fast.compile_object + objdump -t.
- An inline helper's out-of-line weak copy duplicating another unit's:
  port_unit --weak-duplicate <name>.
- Null-first ternary `return !p ? 0 : p->f();` gives `bne call; li 0`.
  `return this == 0 ? -1 : m;` for inline getters with -1 on null.
  Unfolded (index-1)*N addressing: `int i = index - 1;` then arr[i].
- Generator scripts for many tiny weak units: see scratch/game/_frags.json,
  _fv.json, _ls.json patterns in the log (2026-10-07).
- Before drafting a range, check it is uncovered (project units / owned_all.py)
  AND that src/<dir>/<file> does not already exist. The snapshot code_map lists
  ALL ranges, not only uncovered ones. (I once overwrote world_object.cpp.)
- port_unit: always pass `--category reconstructed_game` for src/game and src/ea
  (default is restored_library; --like does not copy it), delete the false
  `adapted_from`, and pass --file-index when a file name has several records.
  Never chain addunit after a port that might fail (it registers missing units).
- fast.py ignores branch targets, relocation names and constant values. Before
  landing, run scratch/rawcmp.py <tmpl> <src> <func> <start> <end> for branch
  targets, and check float literals bit-exactly (objdump -s of .sdata2).
- Vtable slots: `python3 scratch/vtdump.py __vt__<mangled class>`; declare
  virtuals in slot order with `unknownNNN` placeholders. Declare a virtual that
  this TU does not define FIRST in the class so its vtable is not emitted.
- A class whose words precede its vtable pointer: members first (private), then
  public virtuals. Explicit `p->~T()` gives the -1 dtor flag. Declare dtors
  explicitly to avoid implicit dtor emission. Weak functions after first caller.
- CVector3 doubleword copies: wrap an aligned(8) 3-float struct in the class.
  Inline row getters/operators create the stack temps; local declaration order
  sets stack slot order (first declared = highest address).
- Small objects must have their real size (else SDA addressing), e.g.
  g_WeaponFactory 28, CAIFilterGlobal 24, g_Shell 0x19dc.
- DEFERRED-INLINE FILES: if a function inlines a callee that is EMITTED AFTER it in the image, that file is compiled with -inline deferred,auto (reverse emission order). Write the source in reverse image order and use deferred flags (toilet_files.cpp, bsschedule). Re-check parked near-misses with this lens.
- Game profile: GC 1.3.2, -O4,p, -inline auto (not deferred), -Cpp_exceptions
  on, -fp_contract on, -str reuse,readonly. MSSG units use -inline deferred.
- Tools: fast.py, sbs.py, variants.py, climb_decls.py (scratch/game/),
  rawcmp.py, vtdump.py, owned_all.py, dis.sh, land.sh (scratch/).
- fast.py ignores pool constant VALUES; run scratch/one_unit.py <id> (check_pool) before landing.
- Back up scratch every ~3 h:
  `tar czf ~/moh-scratch-backups/scratch-$(date +%Y%m%d-%H%M).tgz --exclude='*.o' --exclude=__pycache__ scratch`

## Rules
- Follow AGENTS.md. Experiments stay in scratch/. Do not invent types, names or
  file boundaries to get a match; mark inferred views/helpers in comments.
- Land only via scratch/land.sh (full reconstruct, snapshot, tests, commit with
  the repo identity and the Co-Authored-By trailer). Never touch orig/ or the
  disc image; no destructive git. No push unless asked.
- Time-box a stubborn function to ~2 iterations, then park it here with the
  draft path, best score and what was tried.
- Pacing in /loop: work to a natural checkpoint, then ScheduleWakeup 60 s.

## Decisions (user, 2026-10-30 ~01:00: "record all of those decisions as agreed")
- APPROVED rule: a plain NAMED COPY of an existing value (e.g. 'T* const self = this;', a typed copy of a parameter, an index local like
  'int i = 0; table[i] = x;') is allowed - it adds no storage, stack slot or code. Devices that ADD storage/stack slots/code stay declined
  (int o[1], padding arrays, STREAM_kill address alias / void* cast). So 02723aaa ChooseCoverPoint + 8b4aca90 MemoryCardLoadGame STAND (pushed),
  and MathLLAngleInit's 'int i = 0' index local (bigfiles trig/perm form, scratch/par/bigfiles/notes.md) is now LANDABLE (not landed yet).
  Supersedes the 2026-10-29 decline of the MathLLAngleInit index local. Re-check staticobjfactory/GetObjInfo forms against this rule
  (GetObjInfo '(int)(filter = 0)' is a chained assignment, not a named copy -> still declined).

- DECLINED: UpdateTargetPosition via a duplicate inline (Update2) differing only in declaration order; clean draft

- FINDING (needs user decision): the 16-byte doubleword struct copies (CVector3 rows in collisionvolume/camera/light/bullet/box/particlesystem/anim_obj) are what GC 1.0-1.2.5 emit for an 8-aligned 4-float struct copy; 1.3+ copy float-wise.
